# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-14T10:58:52+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

Preparing the ExoMars Rosalind Franklin rover for its first steps on Mars ExoMars ©Thales Alenia Space "Every move you make" , the famous chorus of a classic from the past has been resonating in the minds of our technicians while testing every move of Amalia*, the Ground Test Model of the rover, completing its egress test campaign , another important step in preparing the rover for its future journey across the Martian surface. As part of the ESA ExoMars 2028 program , the egress test campaign was conducted by the Thales Alenia Space team, as industrial prime contractor for the ExoMars 2028 mission, at ALTEC’s facilities in Turin at the Rover Operations Control Centre (ROCC), using the mock-up of the Landing Platform supplied by Airbus Defence

- undated — Thales Alenia Space takes off for the 2026 International Astronautical Congress
  Thales Alenia Space takes off for the 2026 International Astronautical Congress | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next CHIME, the Copernicu... From October 5 to 9, Thales Alenia Space will take part in the 77 th International Astronautical Congress (IAC 2026), held in Antalya, Turkey, under the theme “the world needs more space” . The event aims to inspire and raise awareness among the general public and younger generations about the importance of space as a shared resource that must be preserved through international cooperation. On this occasion, Thales Alenia Space will showcase its wide range of technological solutions supporting space exploration, Earth observation, telecommunications and satellite navigation. - undated — CHIME, the Copernicus hyperspectral mission enabling operational global imaging spectroscopy
  CHIME, the Copernicus hyperspectral mission enabling operational global imaging spectroscopy | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next MTG-I2 in the starti... The Copernicus Hyperspectral Imaging Mission for the Environment (CHIME) is advancing its development to be ready for launch. CHIME will provide routine hyperspectral observations from space across the visible to shortwave infrared spectral range (400-2500 nm), supporting enhanced environmental services in areas such as food security, sustainable agriculture, biodiversity monitoring, soil characterisation, mineral resources and water quality. With a swath of approximately 130 km and a spatial sampling distance of 30 m, CHIME will complement Copernicus Sentinel-2 and extend Europe's Earth observation capabilities through operational imaging spectroscopy. - undated — TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators
  TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators TraCSS encourages interested spacecraft operators to sign-up for the&nbsp; Traffic Coordination System for Space &nbsp;(TraCSS). Operators are encouraged to review the&nbsp; TraCSS User Agreement &amp; Data Policy &nbsp;prior to signing up. - undated — Exclusive: Sagittarius Logistics Raises $1.2M to Build Inland Launch Capabilities
  Exclusive: Sagittarius Logistics Raises $1.2M to Build Inland Launch Capabilities Skip to content There’s a new launcher in town. CO-based Sagittarius Logistics, a startup founded last year by former employees of Masten Space Systems, announced the closing of a $1.2M pre-seed round today. The money will fund early development of its reusable-launch program. The round included funds from Nova Threshold, Felicis Scout Fund, Ingenuity VC, and Space Venture Studios. Location, location, location: Sagittarius arose out of the ashes of Masten Space Systems, which went bankrupt in 2022 after going over budget on a lunar lander. Unfortunately, the name Phoenix was already taken.&nbsp; While the IP is all new, Sagittarius was founded by Masten Space Systems’ founder David Masten, and three former Masten employees—CEO Jonathan Slavik, COO Jenna Edwards, and chief of production Daniel Sinkel.   https://payloadspace.com/exclusive-sagittarius-logistics-raises-1-2m-to-build-inland-launch-capabilities/
- undated — Canadian Lunar Rovers Ready for Next Phase—And An Earlier Launch
  Canadian Lunar Rovers Ready for Next Phase—And An Earlier Launch Skip to content Three Canadian companies are racing Moon-cart concepts. Canadensys, MDA Space, and Mission Control collectively received C$14.6M ($10.3M) in July 2025 for a Phase 0 lunar-utility-rover study supporting Artemis. And now, work is almost done. Phase shift: This year, NASA unveiled its plan to build a Moon Base —an undertaking that will require a transportation network that includes big rovers. As a result, CSA is expected to boost funding for its rover projects, and a new RFP should drop within days that will downselect to two companies to keep building in 2027 alongside science-instrument procurements.   https://payloadspace.com/canadian-lunar-rovers-ready-for-next-phase-and-an-earlier-launch/

  Gama Raises €15M Seed to Boost Europe’s Solar Array Supply Chain Skip to content Europe continues to stack the building blocks for a more robust space economy.&nbsp; Gama, a French startup developing ultra-light deployable solar arrays, announced a €15M seed round yesterday to boost Europe’s solar array supply chain. The round included funds from a mix of European and US VCs, including Transition Ventures, Planet A, Draper Associates, and Manta Ray. The funds will be used to develop a high-volume production facility outside of Paris, France, which will be capable of producing 100 MW of solar arrays annually.&nbsp; The first units—called Wings—are expected to start rolling off the line next year.&nbsp; Gama also plans to hire ~60 employees in engineering roles across mechanical, electrical, and software.   NASA Looking for New Ways to Return Perseverance Samples From Mars &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com In January, NASA will begin assessing new strategies to return the samples now being collected by the Mars Perseverance rover to Earth. The 2025-26 Departmental Results Report , posted Nov. 6, is the Canadian Space Agency&#8217;s annual report that outlines what it spent in its fiscal year, which, like those of all government departments, ends on March 31. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

  Another early preview of the Didymos binary asteroid system from ESA’s Hera mission for planetary defence. Acquired from 56 km away, this image shows one face of the main 780-m-diameter Didymos asteroid. A small part of its 151-m-diameter Dimorphos moonlet is also visible, peeking out from near the top of Didymos.

EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . - undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/

- undated — Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick
  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

- Wed, 11 Nov 2026 12:36:39 GMT — Stellenanzeige: Das brauchen Sie, um Astronaut zu werden
  Wer schon immer mal zum Mars oder auf den Mond wollte, kann sich jetzt bei der NASA bewerben. Die Liste der Anforderungen ist streng – und man muss einiges über sein Privatleben offenlegen.

- Sat, 14 Nov 2026 08:27:51 GMT — Starship-Testflug: Fangversuch des Starship-Boosters gescheitert
  Elon Musks Unternehmen SpaceX wollte erstmals beide Raketenstufen des Starship nach dem Start landen lassen. Bei der unteren Stufe hat dies nicht geklappt. Die obere Stufe ist noch unterwegs.

  --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ).   Monaco à la conquête de l&#039;espace --> Sections --> Découvrir Monaco Célébrités Culture Évènements Informations Locales Monaco Dans Le Monde Sortir Structures Sportives Tourisme --> Rejoignez nos 150 000 followers ! Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte
  https://www.monaco-tribune.com/2021/10/lespace-nouveau-centre-dinteret-economique-pour-monaco/

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. - undated — Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo
  Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo | GOV.SI Domov Področja Podjetništvo in gospodarstvo Inovativnost in tehnološki razvoj Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo Slovenija je od 1. januarja 2025 polnopravna članica Evropske vesoljske agencije (angleško The European Space Agency – Esa), kar slovenskim podjetjem in razvojnoraziskovalnim institucijam omogoča sodelovanje pri projektih Ese na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Sodelujejo lahko podjetja in institucije, ki se ukvarjajo z inovativnimi materiali, tehnologijami, procesi ali informacijskimi rešitvami. Priložnost za sodelovanje z Eso imajo tudi mala in srednja podjetja, kar potrjujejo uspešni projekti podjetij iz Slovenije, ki so to priložnost že izkoristila.   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Dr. Farhan: The Kuwait National Space Research Center is a national platform for excellence in space research. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. O documento foi assinado pelo Director-Geral do GGPEN, Dr. Zolana Rui João, e pelo Director-Geral da Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos, Eng. - undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate

  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. #### Royal Centre for Remote Sensing (CRTS) (space_office, fr, text-reader)

- undated — Centre Royal de Télédétection Spatiale
  ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma

- undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  Épargne : gare aux structures non agréées - République Togolaise Tendances: Diplomatie Lire la suite Les arnaques sont fréquentes © republicoftogo.com Le ministre des Finances et du Budget, Essowè Georges Barcola, a publié vendredi un communiqué pour alerter sur la multiplication des structures qui collectent l’épargne et accordent des crédits sans autorisation. Il rappelle que la loi du 24 mars 2026 portant réglementation de la microfinance encadre ces opérations. Selon son article 14, la collecte de dépôts et l’octroi de prêts sont des activités réglementées qui ne peuvent être exercées sans l’autorisation préalable du ministre chargé des Finances. Le ministre met à nouveau en garde les promoteurs de ces structures illégales. Il les prévient qu’ils « s’exposent à la rigueur de la loi ».   https://www.republicoftogo.com/toutes-les-rubriques/eco-finance/epargne-gare-aux-structures-non-agreees

- undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html
- undated — Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India.
  Hosting of 20th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. The meeting had in-person participation of 263 delegates, including 176 foreign delegates from 20 countries, and online participation of 84 delegates. The delegates i
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Hosting_of_20th_International_Committee_on_GNSS.html
- undated — Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully
  Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far. The configuration has been strengthened and essential improvements have been carried out in the nozzle configuration to improve design margins along with updated procedures in the acceptance

  Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added.   WASHINGTON — The US Federal Aviation Administration lifted a nationwide ground stop on Friday after a communications failure at air traffic control centers caused chaos at airports and triggered the longest nationwide shutdown since the September 11, 2001, attacks. Transportation Secretary Sean Duffy said US airspace reopened at 8:15 p.m. Eastern Time (0115 GMT), but warned that departures would resume gradually and it would take time for airlines to restore normal operations. "It's going to be slow," Duffy told reporters at the White House. More than 6,600 flights were canceled and 8,600 delayed, with another 1,500 already canceled for Sunday, according to FlightRadar24.   지구 14바퀴 ‘코끼리코’ 돌고 ‘젓가락 잡기’ 2단 회수…머스크의 ‘무한도전’ 본문 미래&amp;과학 기술 지구 14바퀴 ‘코끼리코’ 돌고 ‘젓가락 잡기’ 2단 회수…머스크의 ‘무한도전’ 곽노필 기자 수정 2026-11-14 19:21 펼침 0:00 Your browser does not support the audio element. 곽노필의 미래창 내일 아침 스타십 15차 시험발사 사상 첫 ‘2단 로켓 회수’ 도전 스페이스엑스의 역대 최강 로켓 스타십이 13일 오후(한국시각 14일 오전) 실시한 15차 시험발사에서 이륙하고 있다. 웹방송 갈무리 광고 일론 머스크가 이끄는 미국의 우주기업 스페이스엑스의 사상 첫 로켓 전체 회수 도전은 무산됐다. 스페이스엑스는 13일 오후 4시47분(한국시각 14일 오전 7시47분) 미국 텍사스 남부 보카치카 해변의 전용 발사장 스타베이스에서 22시간 궤도 비행을 목표로 역대 최강 로켓 스타십의 15번째 시험발사에 나섰다. 스페이스엑스는 이번 비행에서 발사체 1단인 슈퍼헤비 부스터와 2단 스타십을 모두 발사 지점으로 귀환시켜 회수하는 ‘더블-캐치’를 시도할 예정이었다. 로켓 1단과 2단을 모두 회수하게 되면 로켓을 완전 재사용할 수 있게 돼 발사 비용과 시간을 획기적으로 줄일 수 있다. 광고 스페이스엑스가 계획한 회수 방식은 로켓을 발사 지점으로 되돌려 발사대의 두 기계팔로 젓가락 집듯 포획하는, 이른바 ‘젓가락 회수’다. 그러나 1단 슈퍼헤비는 이륙 6분30초 후 발사대가 아닌 멕시코만(아메리카만) 해상으로 큰 폭발음을 내며 착수했다. 포획 시도를 취소한 이유는 즉각 알려지지 않았다. 스페이스엑스는 지금까지 진행한 14차례 시험비행에서 ‘젓가락 회수’ 방식으로 1단 슈퍼헤비를 3번 성공시킨 바 있다. 광고 광고 다만 이번 비행에서 사상 처음으로 시
